# RS3 secret-scan allow list (modular-repo blueprint RS3 step 2, OL12 D2).
#
# A hit this review accepted, in one of two forms. Blank lines and '#' comments are
# ignored. The second form is what keeps the list from growing with every commit.
#
#   <commit> <path> [<pattern>]
#       A whole-tree hit: the reviewed history the split moved, keyed by the split
#       commit the scan printed and the path. Commits up to the split base keep this
#       key because their trees carry the reviewed fixtures again and again.
#
#   = <path> <pattern> <sha256>
#       An added-line hit after the split base: the path, the pattern's name and the
#       SHA-256 of the matched line. Only the hash is stored, never the line, so the
#       same reviewed line added again stays allowed and a different line written
#       into the same allowed fixture path is a new hit.
#
#   = <path> <file-name|state-folder>
#       An added data-file name or state/ folder after the split base, which carries
#       no line to hash.
#
# Every entry is a made-up test fixture value, a constant name, or a PEM header in a
# redaction test. docs/qa/rs3-secret-scan-2026-10-28.md carries the classification, one
# row per path and pattern.
#
# The split commit these whole-tree entries were reviewed against: muster-split
# 996266fc8c44b95d2b3ec3a2e63f6fecb735646c (305 commits). Pass it as --split-base:
# commits reachable from it are scanned whole-tree, every later commit only for the
# lines it adds.
